- **Big Pic -> the Boss (David and Sofer reading) (6534774141, 22:24:18 UTC by its stamp; read to 6534610047): the last two items owed: NOT_CLOSED 28 is smaller than it looked (exchange needs overlap, so quanta born apart weigh exactly as told apart until their hands first share a Node-clock, and the determinant weighing is one rule for both cases); G2's three items all stand, the density road answers two and the third is a scope.** (1) NOT_CLOSED 28, the conditional hand for quanta born apart (the Boss's 6531570391 item 4): at a pair of clicked places (x, y) the determinant's cross term psi1(x) psi2(y) conj(psi2(x) psi1(y)) is nonzero only where both hands are nonzero at both places, so quanta born apart weigh exactly as told apart until their hands first share a Node-clock, a local event inside the act's cone of one Mesh a round. Measured (scratch, on p11's act and hands): two like quanta laid 12 Node-clocks apart, the first round their hands share a Node-clock and the first round the determinant's weight differs from the told-apart weight are the same, round 3, and before it the two agree exactly. So the record weighs every pair of like clicks by the determinant of the two hands' values at the two clicked places, each value read locally at its own click, and where the hands have not met it reduces to the told-apart weight by itself: no "born together" condition is needed for the weight. What stays OPEN is narrower: after a first click at x the second quantum's hand becomes psi1(x) psi2 - psi2(x) psi1, which where psi2(x) is 0 is psi2 itself, so the update is needed only after a meeting, and it runs along psi1's support, Bell's road (decision 34), the same derivation for quanta born together and born apart; the night's price, no exchange between quanta the backward board has not joined, is nature's exactly wherever the hands have not met and bites only on a second click after a meeting. (2) G2's three items (David's 6530200653) against the density road the morning list now carries: all three stand as found. The physicist's N1 (a fixed -1/12 at a nonzero rest): on the density road the push is in the Node-clock's own units by construction, term 5's books' form pushing each slot by minus the derivative of the act's own energy there, which is the physicist's case B, for which David found gamma_obs = beta_obs = 1 at any background. The physicist's N2 (the teeth factor in G) belongs to the slot reading, a source in fixed units: with the push in local units the factor does not arise, so G read by local clocks and rods does not depend on the background. The mathematician's N1 (Postulate 6's content is beta = 1, a 1/r cross part for two bodies) is a scope: for given wells the product rule is beta = 1 and the U^2 terms of two wells compose exactly by the product; for a self-gravitating pair the cross part is physical, since in general relativity the sources' mutual energy gravitates (PPN's Phi_2, coefficient 4 in g_00 at beta = gamma = 1), so the text's cross part is to be compared with that term and not with 0; Postulate 6 holds for clocks in given wells and its two-body form owes Phi_2. So G2's fourth text: take the push in local units (the density road), keep N2 as the slot reading's price, and scope Postulate 6 to given wells with Phi_2 as the two-body test. (3) His queue is clear; he keeps watching for new refusals and re-checks each from above. Method, addressed: (a) each quantum's hand read at its own click, the pair's weight from the two clicked values, the push from the act's own energy in the Node-clock's units; (b) the exchange appears at the round the hands first share a Node-clock and not before, and the push in local units leaves gamma_obs and beta_obs at 1 at any background (David's case B); (c) not following: the conditional hand after a click that follows a meeting (Bell's road, OPEN), and Postulate 6 for self-gravitating pairs without general relativity's Phi_2.
